Now, let's design algorithms in code.

Functions top-level no closures/lambda.

- ensure_sequence_from_global: try height_sequence_1.
- rfft_convolution(a): full conv via FFT. Use scipy? Implement custom.

```
def fft_convolve(a,b=None):
    if b is None: b=a
    n=len(a); m=len(b); size=n+m-1; fsize=1<<(size-1).bit_length()
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,fsize)
    fb=np.fft.rfft(b,fsize)
    return np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,fsize)[:size]
```
Need all top-level. Use float64.

- score_from_p(p): q=fft_conv(p); return 2*n*max(q)
- lse_objective_grad_z(z, tau, n maybe): p=softmax(z); q=conv(p); maxq; shifted=(q-maxq)/tau; w=exp(shifted); sum; w/=sum; grad_p=2*corr_valid(w,p); g = p*(grad_p - dot(p,grad_p)); obj = maxq + tau*np.log(sum_exp_shifted) maybe maybe + const? The constant? We need minimize. If tau constant, obj=LSE. But Fτ = τ LSE(q/τ)? Wait previously Fτ = τ log sum exp(q/τ). Let's derive stable: L(q/τ) = maxq/τ + logΣexp((q-maxq)/τ). τ L = maxq + τ logΣexp((q-maxq)/τ). So objective = maxq + τ*logS. Gradient = ∑softmax grad q. Good. We'll return obj, grad. Note obj may be negative if q negative? q nonnegative but softmax if maxq small could obj ~maxq + τ log(1/(2n))? For n large, maybe negative; constant not critical. Or we can subtract constant to keep positive: obj = maxq + tau*np.log(S) - tau*np.log(q.size) maybe. Doesn't matter. For L-BFGS, relative obj changes matter. Use shifted q for numeric.

Compute corr_valid via FFT:
```
def conv_weight_p(w,p):
    # w length 2n-1, p length n. returns c = sum_{m} w[m] p[m]? We need g[l]=sum_{m=0}^{n-1} w[l+m] p[m].
    # Use FFT length >= (2n-1)+(n-1)=3n-2
    L=2*n-1; size=1 << (L+n-2).bit_length()? Actually len(w)=2n-1, len(p)=n-1? q=reverse(p) len n.
    fsize=1 << ((len(w)+len(p)-1)-1).bit_length()
    fw=np.fft.rfft(w,fsize)
    fp=np.fft.rfft(p[::-1],fsize)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fw*fp,fsize)[:len(w)+len(p)-1]
    return c[n-1:n-1+n]  # indices l+n-1 for l=0..n-1
```
Test small: n=2, w=[w0,w1,w2], p=[p0,p1], q=p[::-1]=[p1,p0]. conv c indices:
c[0]=w0 p1
c[1]=w0 p0 + w1 p1

Softmax stable:
```
def softmax(z):
    z = z - z.max()
    expz=np.exp(z)
    return expz/expz.sum()
```

Need design coordinate update routine.

Given a vector a (nonnegative, not necessarily sum1). Score = 2n max_conv / sum^2. We can keep a sum normalized=1 for stable. But homogeneous coordinate update doesn't need fix sum; if we update one coordinate x, sum changes. We can normalize after each coordinate to sum1 to avoid scale drift. Does that alter exact 1D optimum? If we optimize homogeneous F(x) and then normalize all coordinates by S0+x, the resulting relative weights correspond to some x' and scaling; scaling invariant, so optimum x for F equals optimum relative. But fixing scale after each coordinate is okay if we update x in current scale. If we normalize after, other coordinates scale down; next coordinate sees new others. This is equivalent to coordinate descent on simplex? The objective is invariant, so normalizing after each update doesn't change set of normalized sequences where only coordinate l changed (others proportionally adjusted). Is that the same as one coordinate update in simplex? Yes: In simplex, changing coordinate l while preserving sum 1 scales all others by (1-x)/(S0) relative. But our homogeneous update changes l and leaves others fixed, then normalizes; resulting others are scaled by S/(S+x). This is exactly varying x with others rescaled to sum1. So correct.

    import time
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    deadline = time.monotonic()+budget_s-20
    best_a=None; best_sc=np.inf
    # load prev

    except:

   if sweep_improve < 1e-10: break
   # optional reheat: if small improvement, perturb slightly? Could create local escape: a += small noise, normalize, q update, then continue? But may increase score. Use only if time left and few sweeps.

Let's design `candidate_scores` top-level:
```
def coordinate_candidate_scores(base_slice, b_slice, base_diag, diag_local_idx, max_out, S0, n, xs):
    # xs 1d
    arr = base_slice[None,:] + 2.0 * xs[:,None] * b_slice[None,:]
    arr[:, diag_local_idx] = base_diag + xs*xs
    local = np.max(arr, axis=1)
    total = np.maximum(local, max_out)
    denom = (S0 + xs)**2
    return 2.0 * n * total / denom
```
Need if base_slice length n, diag_local_idx=l. `arr[:,l]` assignment. If xs size 1? okay.

   ...

Full convolution via FFT:
```
def full_convolve(a):
    n=a.size
    size=2*n-1
    fsize=1 << (size-1).bit_length()
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, fsize)
    return np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, fsize)[:size]
```

def score_from_a_q(a,q):
    S=a.sum()
    if S<1e-12: return np.inf
    return 2.0*a.size*float(np.max(q))/ (S*S)
```

```
def coord_sweep(a, q, time_budget, rng, max_sweeps=200, verbose=False):
    n=a.size
    a=a.astype(np.float64).copy()
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<1e-12: ...
    a/=s
    q=full_convolve(a)
    score=2.0*n*float(np.max(q))
    best_a=a.copy(); best_q=q.copy(); best_sc=score
    sweep=0
    stall=0
    t0=time.monotonic()
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_budget:
       sweep+=1
       # recompute q/score from best? use current a maybe best? We optimize current; if current not best due perturbation? We'll keep current=best at start and after perturb.
       q=full_convolve(a); score=2*n*max(q)
       if verbose: print sweep score
       prev_sc=score
       order = rng.permutation(n)
       for l in order:
           new_sc = coord_update_one(a,q,l,score)
           if new_sc < score - 1e-12:
              score = new_sc
           # else score unchanged
       # after sweep
       a_norm = a/a.sum(); q=full_convolve(a_norm); score=2n*max(q)
       if score < best_sc - 1e-12:
           best_sc=score; best_a=a_norm.copy(); best_q=q.copy(); stall=0
       else:
           stall +=1
           if stall >=3 and time...:
               # perturbation
               a = best_a + 0.03*rng.standard_normal(n)
               a = np.clip(a,0,None); a += 1e-8; a/=a.sum(); q=full_convolve(a); score=...
               if score > best_sc*1.1: # if too bad, reduce perturbation or restart? 
                   a=best_a.copy()
               stall=0
       if max_sweeps and sweep>=max_sweeps: break
       # optional: if best improved and stall 0, continue; if no sweep improvement for 5 breaks? but perturbation handles.
    return best_a,best_q,best_sc
```

```
def init_hierarchical(n,rng):
   K = max(8, min(n, int(n*0.25)+rng.integers(0,5)))
   pos = rng.choice(n, size=K, replace=False)
   exponent = np.arange(K, dtype=float)
   rho = 0.5 + 0.4*rng.random()
   w = rho ** exponent
   # add noise
   w *= np.ones(K) + 0.3*rng.standard_normal(K); w=np.abs(w)+1e-4
   a=np.zeros(n); a[pos]=w; a/=a.sum()
   return a
```
This gives top ~1/sum = 1/(K(1-rho)/(1-rho^?)) maybe. For K=32,rho=0.8 sum≈25, top~0.04, below sqrt t for n=128 0.076; okay.

Let's write `eval_candidates` top-level:
```
def eval_coord_candidates(base_slice, lin_slice, base_diag, diag_lo, max_out, S0, n, xs):
    xs = np.asarray(xs, dtype=np.float64)
    if xs.size == 0: return xs, np.array([])
    M=xs.size
    arr = base_slice[np.newaxis, :] + xs[:, np.newaxis] * lin_slice[np.newaxis, :]
    if diag_lo >=0 and diag_lo < base_slice.size:
        arr[:, diag_lo] = base_diag + xs*xs
    local = np.max(arr, axis=1)
    if max_out > local:
       total = np.maximum(local, max_out)
    else: total = local
    denom = (S0 + xs)**2
    scores = (2.0*n) * total / denom
    return xs, scores
```
If `max_out` zero, np.maximum still.

def make_candidates(U, x0, M):
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, M)
    xs = U * t * t
    # include possibly around x0: add x0/2, x0, x0*2 clipped
    xs = np.concatenate((xs, np.array([0.0, U, max(0.0,x0), max(0.0,min(U,x0+1e-8)), max(0.0,min(U,x0-1e-8))])))
    xs = xs[(xs >= 0.0) & (xs <= U)]
    if xs.size ==0: xs=np.array([0.0])
    xs = np.unique(xs)
    return xs
```

def init_hierarchical(n, rng):
    K = int(max(8, min(n, n*0.25 + rng.integers(0, max(1, n//20)))))
    if K == n: pos = np.arange(n)

    order = reversed? We want assign weights to random positions; no need.
    rho = 0.55 + 0.35*rng.random()
    ks = np.arange(K, dtype=float)
    w = rho ** ks
    # make top maybe larger: raise to 1.5? w = (rho**ks)**1.5
    w = w * (1.0 + 0.3*np.abs(rng.standard_normal(K)))
    w = np.maximum(w, 1e-5)
    a = np.zeros(n); a[pos]=w; a /= a.sum()

```
def spread_positions(n, K, rng, n_cand=200):
    K = min(K,n)
    pos=[]
    counts=np.zeros(2*n-1, dtype=np.float64)
    for _ in range(K):
        if n - len(pos) <= n_cand:
            cand_options = np.array([i for i in range(n) if i not in pos])? Avoid set overhead. Use rng.choice(n, size=min(n_cand, n-len(pos)), replace=False); if duplicates with pos possible since choice without replacement from full n can include existing. Filter unique not in pos.
        else:
            cands = rng.choice(n, size=n_cand, replace=False)
        # filter duplicates with pos: for large, use loop; K small.
        filtered=[]
        for c in cands:
           if c not in pos: filtered.append(c)
        if not filtered: take any not in pos

           mx = counts[2*c] + 1.0
           # To reduce Python, loop pos list
           for p in pos:
               s=c+p
               v=counts[s]+2.0
               if v>mx: mx=v
           if mx < best_mx:
              best_mx=mx; best_sum=counts.sum()??
              best_c=c
              if best_mx <=2: maybe break? We could still search for lower but 1 ideal. If mx==1 (only diagonal) can't get lower; break.
        pos.append(best_c)
        counts[2*best_c]+=1
        for p in pos[:-1]: counts[best_c+p]+=2
    return np.array(pos,int)
```

def make_initialization(kind, n, rng, prev_a):
   if kind == 'prev':
       if prev_a is not None and prev_a.size == n: return prev_a.copy()
       elif prev_a is not None: return resize_sequence(prev_a,n)
       else: return init_noisy_uniform(n,rng)
   elif kind == 'prev_noise':
       base = prev_a if prev_a.size==n else resize
       a = base + 0.05*rng.standard_normal(n)
       a = np.clip(a,0,None)+1e-8; return a/a.sum()
   elif kind == 'dirichlet':
       alpha = [0.7,1.0,2.0,0.4] maybe rng choose
       return rng.dirichlet(np.full(n, alpha))
   elif kind == 'spread': return init_spread_weighted(n,rng)
   elif kind == 'exponential': rate = (rng.random()*6-3); if abs<0.3 sign; arange; a=np.exp(rate*i/(n-1)); a+=1e-4; normalize
   elif kind == 'noisy_uniform': a=np.ones(n)+0.8*rng.standard_normal(n); a=np.abs(a)+1e-3; normalize
   elif kind == 'power': alpha=rng.random()*4-1; a=(np.arange(n)+1)**alpha; a=np.abs(a)+1e-3; normalize
```

Resize function:
```
def resize_sequence(old, new_n):
   if old is None: return None
   old_n=old.size
   if old_n == new_n: return old.copy()
   old_x = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,old_n)
   new_x = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,new_n)
   a = np.interp(new_x, old_x, old)
   a = np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0) + 1e-8
   return a/a.sum()
```
Maybe interpolation fills all positions positive; good warm start.

def coord_sweep(a, q, time_budget, rng, perturb_scale=0.02, fine=False, max_sweeps=1000):
    a=normalize...
    q=convolve_sequence(a); score=...
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=score
    if fine: m1=... else m1=...
    stall=0
    t0=...
    sweep=0
    while time...:
       sweep+=1
       q=convolve_sequence(a); score=2n*max(q)
       if score < best_sc: update
       prev=score
       order=rng.permutation(n)
       stopped = False
       for idx,l in enumerate(order):
           new_sc=coord_update_one(a,q,l,score,m1,mref)
           if new_sc < score:
               score=new_sc
               if score < best_sc:
                   best_sc=score; best_a=a.copy(); best_q=conv? maybe q.copy()
           if (idx & 63)==0 and time.monotonic()-t0 >= time_budget:
               stopped=True; break
       if stopped: break
       # after sweep, recomput q from a
       a=a/a.sum(); q=convolve_sequence(a); score=...
       if score < best_sc: update
       if abs(prev-score) < 1e-10:
          stall+=1
          if stall > 2:
             if perturb_scale >0 and time left:
                # perturb best
                a = best_a + perturb_scale*rng.standard_normal(n)
                a = np.maximum(a,0.0) + 1e-8
                a /= a.sum()
                q=convolve_sequence(a)
                score=...
                # If perturbation too bad, reduce? maybe continue
                stall=0
             elif not allow? break
       else: stall=0
       if sweep>=max_sweeps: break
    return best_a, convolve_sequence(best_a), best_sc
```

Now, coord_update_one score computation: It uses `score` passed but also recomputes from q for U? Let's code:
```
def coord_update_one(a, q, l, score, m1=36, mref=14):
   n=a.size
   x0=float(a[l])
   S0=1.0-x0
   if S0 < 1e-14: return score
   mq = float(np.max(q))
   # Since sum1, current score =2n*mq. Use score_for_T = 2n*mq maybe.
   T = mq
   if T <= 1e-15: return score
   sqrtT = np.sqrt(T)
   if sqrtT < 0.999:
      U = sqrtT*S0/(1.0-sqrtT)
   else:
      U = 1.0 + 10.0*S0
   if U < x0: U = x0
   U *= 1.05
   if U <= 1e-14: return score
   start=l; end=l+n
   base_slice = q[start:end].astype(np.float64, copy=True)
   if x0 != 0.0:
      base_slice -= 2.0*x0*a
      base_slice[l] += x0*x0
   base_slice = np.maximum(base_slice,0.0)
   base_diag = float(base_slice[l])
   lin_slice = 2.0*a.copy()
   lin_slice[l]=0.0
   # max_out
   max_left = float(q[:l].max()) if l>0 else 0.0
   max_right = float(q[end:].max()) if end < len(q) else 0.0
   max_out = max(max_left, max_right, 0.0)
   # candidates
   xs1 = make_candidates(U, x0, m1)
   _, sc1 = eval_coord_candidates(base_slice, lin_slice, base_diag, l, max_out, S0, n, xs1)
   best_idx = int(np.argmin(sc1))
   # refine
   if xs1.size >=2:
      if best_idx==0: lo=0.0; hi=xs1[1]
      elif best_idx==xs1.size-1: lo=xs1[-2]; hi=xs1[-1]
      else: lo=xs1[best_idx-1]; hi=xs1[best_idx+1]
      if hi <= lo: hi=lo+max(1e-12, 1e-6*U)
      xs2 = np.linspace(lo,hi,mref)
      xs2 = np.append(xs2, xs1[best_idx])
      xs2 = np.unique(xs2)
      xs2, sc2 = np.sort(xs2), ... cannot sort scores; use eval then argmin.
      _, sc2=eval...
      best_idx2=int(np.argmin(sc2))
      # second refine
      lo2=... based on sc2/xs2
      xs3=np.linspace(lo2,hi2, min(mref,10)); append best; eval; choose
   else xs3...
   total_best_sc = min(sc1[best_idx], sc2[best_idx2], sc3[best_idx3])
   total_best_x = ...
   if total_best_sc < score - 1e-12:
      # update
   return best score
```

```
def make_candidates(U, x0, M):
    if U <= 0: return np.array([0.0])
    t=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,M)
    xs=U*t*t
    extra = np.array([0.0, U, x0])
    # include x0*0.25,0.75,1.5 clipped
    if x0>0:
       extra = np.concatenate((extra, [x0*0.25, x0*0.75, x0*1.5, x0*0.05, x0*2.0]))
    xs=np.concatenate((xs,extra))
    xs=xs[(xs>=0)&(xs<=U)]
    if xs.size==0: return np.array([0.0,U])
    return np.unique(xs)
```

Now, test mentally some helper syntax.

`convolve_sequence`:
```
def convolve_sequence(a):
    n=a.size
    if n == 0: return np.array([0.0])
    if n <= 2048:
        c = np.convolve(a, a)
    else:
        size = 2*n-1
        fsize = 1 << (size-1).bit_length()
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, fsize)
        c = np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, fsize)[:size]
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c
```
If using `out=c`, c dtype float64. okay.

`normalize_nonneg`:
```
def normalize_nonneg(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(s): return None
    a /= s
    return a
```

`score_from_norm` not needed.

`resize_sequence`: If old sum maybe not1? normalize first.
```
def resize_sequence(old, new_n):
    if old is None: return None
    old=np.asarray(old,dtype=np.float64)
    old_norm=normalize_nonneg(old)
    if old_norm is None: return None
    m=old_norm.size
    if m == new_n: return old_norm.copy()
    old_x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m)
    new_x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,new_n)
    a=np.interp(new_x, old_x, old_norm)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0) + 1e-10
    return a/a.sum()
```

Init functions:
```
def init_noisy_uniform(n,rng, noise=0.8):
    a=np.ones(n,dtype=float) + noise*rng.standard_normal(n)
    a=np.abs(a)+1e-4
    return a/a.sum()
```
Maybe use several noise scales. For kind, choose random noise from [0.3,1.5].
```
def init_dirichlet(n,rng):
    alpha = float(rng.choice([0.5,0.7,1.0,1.5,2.0]))
    a=rng.dirichlet(np.full(n,alpha))
    return a
```
For n large, alpha small can produce extreme zeros; coordinate handles. But if many zeros, sum exact from Dirichlet? Dirichlet returns positive sum1.
```
def init_exponential(n,rng):
    rate = (rng.random()*6.0 - 3.0)
    if abs(rate)<0.3: rate = 0.3*np.sign(rate or 1)
    i=np.arange(n, dtype=float)/(max(n-1,1))
    a=np.exp(rate*n*i)  # maybe scale rate*n? Use rate in [-3,3]
    a = a + 1e-6
    return a/a.sum()
```
If rate large negative, first value1 last e^-3=0.05, okay. For n large, use i=0..1.

alpha=rng.random()*4-1.0
i=np.arange(n)+1.0
a=np.power(i, alpha)
a=np.abs(a)+1e-4
return a/a.sum()
```
Could produce inf if alpha large? alpha up to3, n=1024 =>1e9 fine.

Use `n_cand=120` for large. We'll implement.
```
def spread_positions(n, rng, K=None, n_cand=150):
   if K is None: K = min(max(8, int(n*0.2)), 80)
   K=min(K,n)
   pos=[]
   counts=np.zeros(2*n-1, dtype=float)
   for step in range(K):
      if n - len(pos) <= n_cand:
         # create array of not in pos? Maybe use list comprehension
         cands = np.array([i for i in range(n) if i not in pos], dtype=int)
      else:
         cands = rng.choice(n, size=n_cand, replace=False)
         filt=[]
         for c in cands:
            if c not in pos: filt.append(int(c))
         if filt: cands=np.array(filt)
         else: cands=np.array([i for i in range(n) if i not in pos])
      best_c=int(cands[0]); best_mx=np.inf
      # For speed, convert pos to list of int
      for c in cands:
         c=int(c)
         mx=counts[2*c]+1.0
         for p in pos:
            v=counts[c+p]+2.0
            if v>mx: mx=v
         if mx < best_mx:
            best_mx=mx; best_c=c
            if best_mx <= 1.0 + 1e-12: break
      pos.append(best_c)
      counts[2*best_c]+=1.0
      for p in pos[:-1]:
         counts[best_c+p]+=2.0
   return np.array(pos, dtype=int)
```
Note `i not in pos` for list O(K). Fine.
In weights init:
```
def init_spread_weighted(n,rng):
   K = min(max(8, int(n*0.2)), 80)
   pos=spread_positions(n,rng,K)
   K=pos.size
   rho = 0.55 + 0.4*rng.random()
   w = rho ** np.arange(K, dtype=float)
   w = np.maximum(w, 1e-4)
   # optional noise
   w = w * (1.0 + 0.2*rng.standard_normal(K)); w=np.abs(w)+1e-5
   a=np.zeros(n)
   # assign weights to pos in selection order but maybe random permutation of weights? Use selection order.
   for idx,p in enumerate(pos): a[p]=w[idx]
   a += 0.0? zeros
   return a/a.sum()
```

```
def eval_coord_candidates(base_slice, lin_slice, base_diag, diag_local, max_out, S0, n, xs):
    xs = np.asarray(xs, dtype=np.float64)
    if xs.size == 0:
        return np.array([0.0]), np.array([np.inf])
    arr = base_slice[np.newaxis, :] + xs[:, np.newaxis] * lin_slice[np.newaxis, :]
    if 0 <= diag_local < base_slice.shape[0]:
        arr[:, diag_local] = base_diag + xs*xs
    local = np.max(arr, axis=1)
    if max_out > 0.0:
       # can't vector branch? use np.maximum
       total = np.maximum(local, max_out)
    else:
       total = local
    total = np.maximum(total, 0.0)
    denom = (S0 + xs) * (S0 + xs)
    scores = (2.0 * float(n)) * total / denom
    return xs, scores
```

def init_cosine(n,rng):
   f = 1 + int(rng.integers(0,3))
   amp = 0.3+0.6*rng.random()
   ph = 2π*rng.random()
   i=np.arange(n)
   a = (1.0 + amp*np.cos(2π*f*i/(n-1)+ph))**2 + 1e-3
   return a/a.sum()
```

```python
"""
Search for non-negative sequences minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)**2.

The main method is a globally-informed block-coordinate descent in the
scale-invariant simple. For a fixed coordinate x and all other coordinates fixed,
the objective is a one-dimensional max-of-rationals problem. Each coordinate is
minimized by dense candidate generation plus local refinement.  Multiple
sequence lengths and diverse initializations are explored, followed by final
coordinate polish.
"""

import time
import numpy as np


def convolve_sequence(a):
    """Full linear convolution a*a, clipped to nonnegative values."""
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)

    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 512:
        c = np.convolve(a, a)
    else:
        size = 2 * n - 1
        fsize = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, fsize)
        c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, fsize)[:size]

    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c


def normalize_nonneg(a):
    """Clip to [0, 1000] and normalize the sum to 1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return None
    a /= s
    return a


def score_normalized(a, q):
    """Score assuming q = conv(a,a) and sum(a) is 1."""
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * np.max(q))


def resize_sequence(old, new_n):
    """Linearly resize a normalized sequence to a new length."""
    old = normalize_nonneg(old)
    if old is None:
        return None
    m = int(old.size)
    if m == int(new_n):
        return old.copy()
    if m < 2:
        a = np.ones(int(new_n), dtype=np.float64)
        return a / a.sum()
    old_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    new_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(new_n))
    a = np.interp(new_x, old_x, old)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0) + 1e-10
    return a / a.sum()


def init_noisy_uniform(n, rng, noise=None):
    """Nearly uniform positive sequence with random noise."""
    if noise is None:
        noise = 0.3 + 1.2 * float(rng.random())
    a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) + noise * rng.standard_normal(n)
    a = np.abs(a) + 1e-4
    return a / a.sum()


def init_dirichlet(n, rng):
    """Random Dirichlet simplex point."""
    alpha = float(rng.choice(np.array([0.5, 0.7, 1.0, 1.5, 2.5], dtype=np.float64)))
    a = rng.dirichlet(np.full(n, alpha, dtype=np.float64))
    return a.astype(np.float64)


def init_exponential(n, rng):
    """Monotone exponential profile."""
    rate = float(rng.uniform(-4.0, 4.0))
    if abs(rate) < 0.5:
        rate = 1.0 if rate >= 0.0 else -1.0
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(n))
    a = np.exp(rate * 3.0 * t)
    a = a + 1e-6
    return a / a.sum()


def init_power(n, rng):
    """Power-law profile."""
    alpha = float(rng.uniform(-1.5, 3.0))
    i = np.arange(int(n), dtype=np.float64) + 1.0
    a = np.power(i, alpha)
    a = np.abs(a) + 1e-4
    return a / a.sum()


def init_cosine(n, rng):
    """Squared cosine profile; can be strongly asymmetric."""
    f = 1 + int(rng.integers(0, 4))
    amp = 0.3 + 0.7 * float(rng.random())
    phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
    i = np.arange(int(n), dtype=np.float64)
    denom = max(int(n) - 1, 1)
    a = (1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * i / denom + phase)) ** 2
    a = a + 1e-3
    return a / a.sum()


def spread_positions(n, rng, K=None, n_cand=160):
    """
    Greedy low-collision support positions.
    Tries to keep ordered pair-sum multiplicities small.
    """
    n = int(n)
    if K is None:
        K = min(max(8, int(0.20 * n)), 80)
    K = int(min(max(1, K), n))

    pos = []
    counts = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)

    for _ in range(K):
        remaining = n - len(pos)
        if remaining <= 0:
            break

        if remaining <= n_cand:
            cand = np.array([i for i in range(n) if i not in pos], dtype=int)
        else:
            raw = rng.choice(n, size=n_cand, replace=False)
            filt = []
            for c in raw:
                ci = int(c)
                if ci not in pos:
                    filt.append(ci)
            if filt:
                cand = np.array(filt, dtype=int)
            else:
                cand = np.array([i for i in range(n) if i not in pos], dtype=int)

        if cand.size == 0:
            break

        best_c = int(cand[0])
        best_mx = np.inf

        for c in cand:
            c = int(c)
            mx = float(counts[2 * c]) + 1.0
            for p in pos:
                val = float(counts[c + p]) + 2.0
                if val > mx:
                    mx = val
            if mx < best_mx:
                best_mx = mx
                best_c = c
                if best_mx <= 1.0 + 1e-12:
                    break

        pos.append(best_c)
        counts[2 * best_c] += 1.0
        for p in pos[:-1]:
            counts[best_c + p] += 2.0

    return np.array(pos, dtype=int)


def init_spread_weighted(n, rng):
    """
    Sparse hierarchical initialization on a low-collision support.
    """
    n = int(n)
    K = min(max(8, int(0.20 * n)), 80)
    pos = spread_positions(n, rng, K=K, n_cand=160)
    K = int(pos.size)
    if K == 0:
        return np.ones(n) / n

    rho = 0.55 + 0.40 * float(rng.random())
    w = rho ** np.arange(K, dtype=np.float64)
    w = w * (1.0 + 0.25 * rng.standard_normal(K))
    w = np.abs(w) + 1e-5

    a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[pos] = w
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        return a / n
    return a / s


def make_initialization(kind, n, rng, prev_a):
    """Create an initial normalized nonnegative sequence."""
    n = int(n)
    if kind == "prev" or kind == "prev_noise":
        if prev_a is not None and prev_a.size == n:
            base = prev_a.copy()
        elif prev_a is not None:
            base = resize_sequence(prev_a, n)
            if base is None:
                base = init_noisy_uniform(n, rng)
        else:
            base = init_noisy_uniform(n, rng)

        if kind == "prev_noise":
            scale = 0.02 + 0.06 * float(rng.random())
            a = base + scale * rng.standard_normal(n)
            a = np.maximum(a, 0.0) + 1e-8
            a /= a.sum()
            return a
        return base

    if kind == "dirichlet":
        return init_dirichlet(n, rng)

    if kind == "spread":
        return init_spread_weighted(n, rng)

    if kind == "exponential":
        return init_exponential(n, rng)

    if kind == "power":
        return init_power(n, rng)

    if kind == "cosine":
        return init_cosine(n, rng)

    return init_noisy_uniform(n, rng)


def make_candidates(U, x0, M):
    """Candidate x-values on [0, U], dense near zero."""
    U = float(U)
    if U <= 1e-15:
        return np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)

    M = max(4, int(M))
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, M, dtype=np.float64)
    xs = U * t * t

    extra = np.array([0.0, U, float(x0)], dtype=np.float64)
    if x0 > 0.0:
        extra = np.concatenate((extra, np.array([
            0.05 * x0,
            0.25 * x0,
            0.75 * x0,
            1.5 * x0,
            2.5 * x0,
        ], dtype=np.float64)))

    xs = np.concatenate((xs, extra))
    xs = xs[(xs >= 0.0) & (xs <= U)]
    if xs.size == 0:
        xs = np.array([0.0, U], dtype=np.float64)
    xs = np.unique(xs)
    return xs.astype(np.float64)


def eval_coord_candidates(base_slice, lin_slice, base_diag, diag_local,
                          max_out, S0, n, xs):
    """
    Evaluate the one-coordinate objective for all candidate x.
    base_slice = convolution coefficients b*b on shift [l, l+n).
    lin_slice = 2*b_j.
    """
    xs = np.asarray(xs, dtype=np.float64)
    if xs.size == 0:
        return np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64), np.array([np.inf], dtype=np.float64)

    arr = base_slice[np.newaxis, :] + xs[:, np.newaxis] * lin_slice[np.newaxis, :]
    if 0 <= int(diag_local) < int(base_slice.size):
        arr[:, int(diag_local)] = float(base_diag) + xs * xs

    local_max = np.max(arr, axis=1)
    total_max = np.maximum(local_max, float(max_out))
    total_max = np.maximum(total_max, 0.0)

    denom = (S0 + xs) * (S0 + xs)
    scores = (2.0 * float(n)) * total_max / denom
    return xs, scores.astype(np.float64)


def coord_update_one(a, q, l, score, m1=36, mref=14):
    """
    Optimize one coordinate exactly-ish (one-dimensional search).
    Updates a and q in place if an improvement is found.
    Returns the best known score for the current state.
    """
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return score

    l = int(l)
    if l < 0 or l >= n:
        return score

    x0 = float(a[l])
    S0 = 1.0 - x0
    if S0 <= 1e-14:
        return score

    mq = float(np.max(q))
    if not np.isfinite(mq) or mq <= 1e-15:
        return score

    # Safe box: any point no worse than current must satisfy x^2/(S0+x)^2 <= mq.
    T = mq
    sqrtT = float(np.sqrt(T))
    if sqrtT < 0.999:
        U = sqrtT * S0 / (1.0 - sqrtT)
    else:
        U = 1.0 + 10.0 * S0

    if U < x0:
        U = x0
    U *= 1.05
    if U <= 1e-15:
        return score

    start = l
    end = l + n
    lenq = int(q.size)

    base_slice = q[start:end].astype(np.float64, copy=True)
    if x0 != 0.0:
        base_slice -= 2.0 * x0 * a
        base_slice[l] += x0 * x0
    base_slice = np.maximum(base_slice, 0.0)
    base_diag = float(base_slice[l])

    lin_slice = 2.0 * a.astype(np.float64, copy=True)
    lin_slice[l] = 0.0

    max_left = float(q[:l].max()) if l > 0 else 0.0
    max_right = float(q[end:].max()) if end < lenq else 0.0
    max_out = max(0.0, max_left, max_right)

    xs1 = make_candidates(U, x0, m1)
    _, sc1 = eval_coord_candidates(
        base_slice, lin_slice, base_diag, l, max_out, S0, n, xs1
    )

    cur_xs = xs1
    cur_idx = int(np.argmin(sc1))
    best_x = float(cur_xs[cur_idx])
    best_s = float(sc1[cur_idx])

    for _ in range(2):
        xs = cur_xs
        idx = cur_idx
        if xs.size < 2:
            break

        if idx == 0:
            lo = 0.0
            hi = float(xs[1])
        elif idx == xs.size - 1:
            lo = float(xs[-2])
            hi = float(xs[-1])
        else:
            lo = float(xs[idx - 1])
            hi = float(xs[idx + 1])

        if hi <= lo:
            hi = lo + max(1e-12, 1e-8 * U)

        xs_new = np.unique(np.concatenate((
            np.linspace(lo, hi, max(4, int(mref)), dtype=np.float64),
            np.array([best_x], dtype=np.float64)
        )))
        _, sc_new = eval_coord_candidates(
            base_slice, lin_slice, base_diag, l, max_out, S0, n, xs_new
        )
        idx_new = int(np.argmin(sc_new))
        s_new = float(sc_new[idx_new])

        if s_new < best_s - 1e-15:
            best_s = s_new
            best_x = float(xs_new[idx_new])
            cur_xs = xs_new
            cur_idx = idx_new
        else:
            break

    if best_s >= score - 1e-12:
        return score

    x_new = best_x
    delta = x_new - x0
    Snew = S0 + x_new
    if Snew <= 1e-14:
        return score

    q[start:end] += 2.0 * delta * a
    q[start + l] += delta * delta

    invs = 1.0 / Snew
    q *= invs * invs

    a *= invs
    a[l] = x_new * invs

    np.maximum(a, 0.0, out=a)
    np.maximum(q, 0.0, out=q)

    # Recompute the score from the updated exact (or FFT) convolution.
    new_score = float(2.0 * n * np.max(q))
    if not np.isfinite(new_score):
        return score
    return new_score if new_score < score else score


def coord_sweep(a, q, time_budget, rng, perturb_scale=0.02, fine=False,
                max_sweeps=10000):
    """
    Block-coordinate polishing with periodic random restarts from the current best.
    """
    a = normalize_nonneg(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy())
    if a is None:
        return None, None, np.inf

    n = int(a.size)
    q = convolve_sequence(a)
    score = score_normalized(a, q)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_q = q.copy()
    best_sc = score

    if fine:
        if n <= 128:
            m1, mref = 80, 24
        elif n <= 512:
            m1, mref = 56, 18
        else:
            m1, mref = 36, 14
    else:
        if n <= 128:
            m1, mref = 48, 16
        elif n <= 512:
            m1, mref = 34, 12
        else:
            m1, mref = 24, 9

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    sweep = 0
    stall = 0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < max(0.05, float(time_budget)):
        sweep += 1

        a = a / float(a.sum())
        q = convolve_sequence(a)
        score = score_normalized(a, q)

        if score < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc = score
            best_a = a.copy()
            best_q = q.copy()

        prev_sc = score
        order = rng.permutation(n)

        stopped = False
        for idx, l in enumerate(order):
            new_sc = coord_update_one(a, q, int(l), score, m1=m1, mref=mref)
            if new_sc < score:
                score = new_sc
                if score < best_sc - 1e-12:
                    best_sc = score
                    best_a = a.copy()
                    best_q = convolve_sequence(a)

            if (idx & 63) == 0 and time.monotonic() - t0 >= float(time_budget) - 0.2:
                stopped = True
                break

        if stopped:
            break

        a = a / float(a.sum())
        q = convolve_sequence(a)
        score = score_normalized(a, q)

        if score < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc = score
            best_a = a.copy()
            best_q = q.copy()
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1

        if stall >= 3:
            if perturb_scale > 0.0 and time.monotonic() - t0 < float(time_budget) - 5.0:
                accepted = False
                for ps in (perturb_scale, 0.45 * perturb_scale, 0.15 * perturb_scale):
                    cand = best_a + ps * rng.standard_normal(n)
                    cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0) + 1e-9
                    cand /= cand.sum()
                    cq = convolve_sequence(cand)
                    cs = score_normalized(cand, cq)
                    if np.isfinite(cs) and cs < best_sc + 0.10:
                        a = cand
                        q = cq
                        score = cs
                        accepted = True
                        break
                if not accepted:
                    a = best_a.copy()
                    q = convolve_sequence(a)
                    score = score_normalized(a, q)

                stall = 0
            else:
                break

        if max_sweeps is not None and sweep >= int(max_sweeps):
            break

    best_a = normalize_nonneg(best_a)
    if best_a is not None:
        best_q = convolve_sequence(best_a)
        best_sc = score_normalized(best_a, best_q)

    return best_a, best_q, float(best_sc)


def get_job_budget(n, remaining):
    """Time budget for a search job of a given sequence length."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 128:
        base = 45.0
    elif n <= 256:
        base = 60.0
    elif n <= 512:
        base = 75.0
    elif n <= 1024:
        base = 90.0
    else:
        base = 110.0
    return float(min(base, max(0.0, float(remaining))))


def propose_candidate(seed=20240614, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Search for the best non-negative height sequence under the given objective.
    Returns a Python list of float heights.
    """
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = max(60.0, float(budget_s))
    search_deadline = t_start + 0.82 * total_budget
    final_deadline = t_start + total_budget - 25.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) if seed is not None else 20240614)

    best_a = None
    best_q = None
    best_sc = np.inf

    def consider(a, q, sc, tag):
        nonlocal best_a, best_q, best_sc
        if a is None or q is None:
            return
        sc = float(sc)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
            best_a = normalize_nonneg(best_a)
            best_q = convolve_sequence(best_a)
            best_sc = score_normalized(best_a, best_q)
            print(f"[update] {tag}: n={best_a.size}, score={best_sc:.8f}")

    # Load any available previous construction.
    prev_a = None
    try:
        raw_prev = height_sequence_1  # assumed global in the runner
        hp = np.asarray(raw_prev, dtype=np.float64)
        hp = np.clip(hp, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if hp.ndim == 1 and hp.size >= 2 and float(hp.sum()) > 1e-6 and np.all(np.isfinite(hp)):
            prev_a = normalize_nonneg(hp)
            if prev_a is not None:
                prev_q = convolve_sequence(prev_a)
                prev_sc = score_normalized(prev_a, prev_q)
                best_a = prev_a.copy()
                best_q = prev_q.copy()
                best_sc = prev_sc
                print(f"[init] loaded previous: n={prev_a.size}, score={prev_sc:.8f}")
    except Exception:
        prev_a = None

    # Polish the previous construction first, if it exists.
    if best_a is not None and np.isfinite(best_sc):
        now = time.monotonic()
        pol_time = min(240.0, max(5.0, float(search_deadline - now - 250.0)))
        if pol_time > 5.0:
            a2, q2, sc2 = coord_sweep(
                best_a.copy(), best_q.copy(), pol_time, rng,
                perturb_scale=0.02, fine=False
            )
            consider(a2, q2, sc2, "prev-polish")

    # Sequence lengths to explore. Include the previous length when reasonable.
    base_ns = [96, 128, 160, 192, 256, 384, 512, 768]
    if prev_a is not None and 32 <= int(prev_a.size) <= 2048:
        base_ns.append(int(prev_a.size))
    n_list = sorted(set(int(x) for x in base_ns if 32 <= int(x) <= 2048))

    # If time is limited, avoid overly large lengths.
    if total_budget < 1600.0:
        n_list = [n for n in n_list if n <= 512]

    # Ranked initialization kinds.
    for n in n_list:
        if time.monotonic() > float(search_deadline) - 30.0:
            break

        kinds = []
        if prev_a is not None:
            kinds.append("prev_noise")
        kinds.extend(["dirichlet", "spread", "cosine", "exponential", "power", "noisy_uniform"])

        # More init variants for small lengths, fewer for large lengths.
        if n <= 128:
            kinds = kinds[:6]
        elif n <= 512:
            kinds = kinds[:5]
        else:
            kinds = kinds[:4]

        for kind in kinds:
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now > float(search_deadline) - 20.0:
                break

            tb = get_job_budget(n, float(search_deadline - now - 10.0))
            if tb < 5.0:
                continue

            a0 = make_initialization(kind, n, rng, prev_a)
            if a0 is None:
                continue

            q0 = convolve_sequence(a0)
            sc0 = score_normalized(a0, q0)
            if not np.isfinite(sc0):
                continue

            a2, q2, sc2 = coord_sweep(
                a0.copy(), q0, tb, rng,
                perturb_scale=(0.02 if n <= 512 else 0.012),
                fine=False
            )
            consider(a2, q2, sc2, f"job n={n} kind={kind}")

    # Final final-polish from the best sequence found.
    if best_a is not None and np.isfinite(best_sc) and time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 5.0:
        remain = max(0.0, float(final_deadline - time.monotonic() - 2.0))
        if remain > 10.0:
            t1 = min(remain * 0.55, 180.0)
            a2, q2, sc2 = coord_sweep(
                best_a.copy(), convolve_sequence(best_a), t1, rng,
                perturb_scale=0.012, fine=False
            )
            consider(a2, q2, sc2, "final-perturb-polish")

        remain = max(0.0, float(final_deadline - time.monotonic() - 2.0))
        if remain > 8.0:
            a2, q2, sc2 = coord_sweep(
                best_a.copy(), convolve_sequence(best_a), remain, rng,
                perturb_scale=0.0, fine=True
            )
            consider(a2, q2, sc2, "final-fine-polish")

    # Fallback if something unexpected happened.
    if best_a is None or not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        n_fb = 128
        best_a = np.ones(n_fb, dtype=np.float64) / n_fb
        best_q = convolve_sequence(best_a)
        best_sc = score_normalized(best_a, best_q)

    # Prepare output.
    result = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    result = np.clip(result, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(result.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        result = np.ones(128, dtype=np.float64) / 128.0
        s = 1.0
    else:
        result /= s

    # Clean tiny values.
    result[result < 1e-16] = 0.0
    out_list = [float(x) for x in result]

    internal_sc = score_normalized(result, convolve_sequence(result))
    print(f"[final] n={len(out_list)}, internal={internal_sc:.8f}")

    try:
        eval_sc = evaluate_sequence(out_list)
        print(f"[final] evaluator={float(eval_sc):.8f}")
        if not np.isfinite(eval_sc):
            # Very defensive fallback.
            out_list = [1.0 / 128.0] * 128
            try:
                eval_sc = evaluate_sequence(out_list)
                print(f"[fallback] evaluator={float(eval_sc):.8f}")
            except Exception:
                pass
    except Exception:
        pass

    return out_list
```
